Hosts GATE #3 for final human approval. Never writes any file. Never invoke outside the harness. - **playtest-qa**: Runs the UI in a browser (Playwright) with demo data and reports what a player actually sees: screenshots, console errors, reduced-motion, mobile viewport, frame-rate sample, and whether game pages look different from normal mode. Use after any game-mode UI change and before a PR. Read-only against the app. ([frmingest/Aladdin](https://github.com/frmingest/Aladdin/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtest-qa.md))
- **playtest-qa**: 바뀐 것에 맞는 봇·점검 스크립트를 돌리고 스크린숏을 사람 눈으로 본 뒤, 기준 수치와 견줘 판정을 보고한다. 마일스톤을 끝낼 때, 손맛·화면·흐름이 바뀌었을 때 쓴다. 코드는 고치지 않는다. ([sksskdf/mythic-relics](https://github.com/sksskdf/mythic-relics/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtest-qa.md))
- **playtest-reviewer**: The independent playtest reviewer for a run: builds and plays the game itself through the scripted-input seam, captures its own frame sequences and logs, and scores all six rubric dimensions from what it actually observed. It is never the implementer and is read-only on source. Invoke once per review round with a small handoff (project root, engine binary, build entry, input seam, acceptance criteria, round number). ([HunLuanZhiZhu/Game-Studios-Plugin](https://github.com/HunLuanZhiZhu/Game-Studios-Plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/playtest-reviewer.md) · ★2)
- **playtest-runner**: Runs a long Easy Red 2 play-test unattended — deploy, launch, drive the game, wait on log evidence, and report what the decision trace actually shows. Use when a run will take many minutes and you do not want it occupying the main conversation. ([jflessenkemper/easy-red-2-claude-plugin](https://github.com/jflessenkemper/easy-red-2-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/easy-red-2/agents/playtest-runner.md))

Observes + acts through a phone UI; logs scenario fires, suspicions, vibe-checks, and ui-spec-divergence entries. Strictly confined to MCP Playwright tools + Write. - **playtest-triage**: Diagnoses one BURNED playtest-harness issue seed and writes one issue file (runs/&lt;id&gt;/issues/NNN-&lt;slug&gt;.md). Receives a filled system prompt from the orchestrator (seed-kind-aware per phase-5 D14). Reads session artifacts, source tree, E2E-ISSUE-LIST.md, and RULES-REFERENCE.md. Proposes 1-3 fix paths with tradeoffs; does not implement. Strictly confined to Read / Write / Grep / Glob / Sequential Thinking. ([mbriggsy/ai-learning-journey](https://github.com/mbriggsy/ai-learning-journey/blob/HEAD/projects/burned/.claude/agents/playtest-triage.md))
- **playtest-triage**: Takes one finding from an rpg-playtest run (a failed probe, a puppeting slip, a stock phrase) and checks whether it is real, then finds the code or prompt in the game that caused it and suggests the smallest fix. Read-only. Use one per finding, several in parallel. ([nooram82/ai-rpg-playtest](https://github.com/nooram82/ai-rpg-playtest/blob/HEAD/agents/playtest-triage.md))
- **playtester**: Plays a translated build turn by turn with play.py toward the goal in the dispatch, per docs/playtester-brief.md. ([minhnhattonthat/translate-retro-game](https://github.com/minhnhattonthat/translate-retro-game/blob/HEAD/agents/playtester.md) · ★10 · 4 copies)
- **playtester**: Plays the game over MCP with a short tool list, never reading code. Give it a goal (reach the door, finish the menu); it plays in fixed ticks and reports the bugs it hits. ([mirai-engine/mirai](https://github.com/mirai-engine/mirai/blob/HEAD/templates/common/.claude/agents/playtester.md) · ★8)
- **playtester**: Runs bot playtests against a worktree, judges metrics and screenshots, writes the PASS/FAIL gate verdict, and files issues. Use as the pre-merge gate and for exploratory QA. ([aetherwing-io/hullbreaker](https://github.com/aetherwing-io/hullbreaker/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md) · ★4)
- **playtester**: Plays Magic Merger like a player, in a phone sized headless browser (390 by 844, then the owner's Z Fold folded and unfolded), and reports what a person would notice: bugs with steps to reproduce, items that break the promise of their name, confusion, friction and delight. Use after building anything players see, before marking a pull request ready, and when hunting bugs before the owner finds them. Never touches production. ([angelus76300-afk/magic-merger](https://github.com/angelus76300-afk/magic-merger/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md) · ★1)
- **playtester**: Plays a Godot 4 game off-screen with gdh against a stated goal ("can the player reach the door?", "does the pause menu open and close?", "does the enemy chase the player?") and reports pass or fail with screenshots and numbers. Use it after changing gameplay, controls, UI flow or scenes, to check the change in the running game without cluttering the main conversation. Give it the project directory, the scene (or say the main scene), the goal, and anything it needs to know (input actions, node names, what success looks like). ([astrosteveo/gdh](https://github.com/astrosteveo/gdh/blob/HEAD/agents/playtester.md) · ★1)
- **playtester**: Repeatedly play one Winternight LT level through public inputs and report balance, reliability, soft locks, and player-facing friction without changing game content. ([chrishart0/winternight-rpg](https://github.com/chrishart0/winternight-rpg/blob/HEAD/.omp/agents/playtester.md) · ★1)
- **playtester**: Testing persona that actively plays through the game in its own browser (the playtest session tool), exploring multiple paths and documenting UX friction, flow gaps, and delight moments. Returns a structured playtest report — never modifies code or game data. ([no-trbl-2-u/Miserere-mei-deus](https://github.com/no-trbl-2-u/Miserere-mei-deus/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md) · ★1)
- **playtester**: Player-experience playtester for the-unseen-hand. Delegate to this agent to evaluate how the game FEELS from a player's seat — onboarding, build discovery, difficulty, fun, frustration. Spawn 2–3 instances in parallel with DIFFERENT personas (newcomer / min-maxer / veteran). A complete campaign is playable, so play it — walking the docs is the fallback for content that does not exist yet, not the default. Reports experience, not code fixes, and must say plainly that an agent's read is not a human's. ([Raafiq/the-unseen-hand-turn-based](https://github.com/Raafiq/the-unseen-hand-turn-based/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md) · ★1)
- **playtester**: Use to run a game or app build, play it the way a user would, and get back the defects with the captured frames that show them. ([sinnafuls/azazel-cli](https://github.com/sinnafuls/azazel-cli/blob/HEAD/crates/azazel-team/agents/playtester.md) · ★1)
- **playtester**: Simulates a fresh player's experience through a scene, puzzle, or system to catch design failures before building. Use before implementing any puzzle, Vanguard AI behavior, crossing sequence, or new mechanic. Produces a playtester session report written as if a real person just played through the described experience. ([AloneFancy/kenoma](https://github.com/AloneFancy/kenoma/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Skywalker studio playtester. Plays the game with bots and its own persona (newcomer, speedrunner, explorer) via playtest_run and sim_input, then files honest, specific feedback with evidence. Does not fix things. Use to find difficulty, clarity, fun and bug problems. ([amirhossein-razlighi/Skywalker](https://github.com/amirhossein-razlighi/Skywalker/blob/HEAD/integrations/cursor/.cursor/agents/playtester.md) · 4 copies)
- **playtester**: Plays the game through the headless test kit and screenshots, then writes a player's-eye report. Invoke at the end of milestones M6, M7, M8 and Phase 2 milestones E2, E3b, E5. ([BucketHarmony/rust-mining-co](https://github.com/BucketHarmony/rust-mining-co/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Quick first-time-player check on the real build, by sight only through v4-eyes (screenshots, coordinates and timings, never the DOM). Reports confusion, friction, waits, bugs and fun after UI or rules changes. Never reads the repo. ([cojamz/cults](https://github.com/cojamz/cults/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md) · 2 copies)
- **playtester**: Reviews the built game for correctness, GDD conformance, responsive design, and valid GitHub Pages deployment config. Signs off or returns specific file-and-line issues to the builder for revision. ([gioperalto/harnest](https://github.com/gioperalto/harnest/blob/HEAD/nest/public/webgame/claude/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: ゲームに無作為なキー・クリックを浴びせて、入力が死んでいないか・固まらないか・操作中に例外が出ないかを見るプレイテスト担当。dynamic-tester が「起動して例外が無いか」しか見ないのに対し、こちらは「起動したあと遊べるか」を、種付きの乱数（落ちたら同じ入力列で再現できる）で確かめる。報告のみで直さない。Dynamic-Tester の PASS 後に走る。新作ゲームの公開前、ゲームの入力・状態遷移に手を入れたとき、および「操作が効かない」「固まる」の報告で使う。 ([hifukasawa77-lgtm/main](https://github.com/hifukasawa77-lgtm/main/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Plays a game end-to-end in a real browser via Chrome and reports where it's confusing, boring, or broken, and whether the ending is screenshot-worthy. Use after building or updating a game's core loop, and as part of the pre-ship checklist. ([ItsSebis/spily](https://github.com/ItsSebis/spily/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: USER-PERSPECTIVE playtest gate (the operator 2026-09-06). Use after any build and before prod, and on a cadence against the live site. Plays the product as a real stranger on a phone with the headless-browser harness, reports clutter, confusion, dead ends, and broken flows with screenshots. Never fixes code. ([jasonpalmer1/claude-code-setup](https://github.com/jasonpalmer1/claude-code-setup/blob/HEAD/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Tests a game's rules, loop, and balance by running builds, headless and bot matches, and logs, and by writing plans for human sessions. Use after a playable milestone and before balance changes. Reports findings with repro steps and never fixes them. ([joealfonso/AgentsAndSkills](https://github.com/joealfonso/AgentsAndSkills/blob/HEAD/plugins/game-team/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Lance un playtest dans Roblox Studio via le serveur MCP de Studio, simule des entrées, capture l'écran, lit la console et rapporte les bugs. Ne fonctionne que sur le PC où Studio tourne. ([lalamefine/roblox-cycles](https://github.com/lalamefine/roblox-cycles/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Testing persona that actively plays through the game via Playwright, exploring multiple paths and documenting UX friction, flow gaps, and delight moments. Returns a structured playtest report — never modifies code or game data. ([no-trbl-2-u/Axiomancer](https://github.com/no-trbl-2-u/Axiomancer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Black-box Claude playtester for REBEL_CELL. Plays one full campaign start to finish through screenshots, mouse and keyboard only (tools/playtest/blackbox.py), in a persona and focus rolled by tools/playtest/roll_testers.py, and writes the feedback report. Never reads code or docs. Sonnet, medium effort. ([Noitues/REBEL_CELL](https://github.com/Noitues/REBEL_CELL/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: QA agent that playtests the game inside Roblox Studio through the Studio MCP server, reads console output and reports bugs. Use after a feature passes `lune run check`. ([RustikArt/roblox-starter](https://github.com/RustikArt/roblox-starter/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Plays the Wizardry Empire English build with tools/emu.py toward the goal in the dispatch. Skill: skills/playtester/skill.md. ([ryz/game-hacking](https://github.com/ryz/game-hacking/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Plays the demo as a specific kind of pupil and reports where they get stuck, bored or find a shortcut. Run several in parallel with different personas. ([scriptsherlock/trickbot](https://github.com/scriptsherlock/trickbot/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Use this agent when you want real user feedback on the app's UX, flows, and features. Launch this agent after implementing a new feature, fixing a UI bug, or when you want a fresh perspective on existing screens. The agent uses Chrome MCP to actually navigate and interact with the app.\n\nExamples:\n\n- User: "I just finished the new inventory management feature"\n Assistant: "Let me launch the playtester agent to get real user feedback on this new feature."\n (Uses Agent tool to launch playtester, who navigates to the feature via Chrome MCP, tries to use it naturally, and reports frustration… ([tmouchart/arran-dnd](https://github.com/tmouchart/arran-dnd/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Independent QA playtester. Invoke it before your final answer on any non-trivial build (game, app, website, tool, plugin, 3D model) with the user's ORIGINAL request verbatim. It runs and uses the result itself, judges it like the demanding user, and writes .proof/review.md with a verdict and a prioritized defect list. It never edits the project. ([vanaedynak/proofkit](https://github.com/vanaedynak/proofkit/blob/HEAD/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester**: Adversarial QA playtester for Godot games. Drives the running game with the godot MCP playtest tools (stepped input sequences, state observation, screenshots), hunts for bugs, softlocks, runtime errors and feel/readability problems, and reports findings with evidence. Use after a feature or milestone is implemented, or when the user asks for a playtest. Does not modify project files. ([Yakoub-ai/godot-claude](https://github.com/Yakoub-ai/godot-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/playtester.md))
- **playtester-builder**: Use to test whether Citybuilder punishes or bores a relaxed player who wants a beautiful, orderly town and ignores efficiency. Plays on an iPad in portrait. ([cedricziel/citybuilder](https://github.com/cedricziel/citybuilder/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester-builder.md))
- **playtester-chaos**: Use to try to break Citybuilder: odd orders of actions, demolishing mid-delivery, bankrupting on purpose, edge-case taps, rotation and relaunch mid-action. Reports bugs with exact repeat steps. Plays on an iPhone SE-size screen in portrait. ([cedricziel/citybuilder](https://github.com/cedricziel/citybuilder/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester-chaos.md))
- **playtester-newcomer**: Use to test the first hour of Citybuilder as someone who has never played a city builder: reads every hint literally, gets lost without guidance, quits after about 15 minutes of confusion. Plays on an iPhone in portrait. ([cedricziel/citybuilder](https://github.com/cedricziel/citybuilder/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester-newcomer.md))
- **playtester-optimizer**: Use to stress Citybuilder's economy as a min-maxer: hunts the best production ratios and exploits, probes storage caps and backpressure, and notices numbers that do not add up. Plays on an iPad in landscape. ([cedricziel/citybuilder](https://github.com/cedricziel/citybuilder/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester-optimizer.md))
- **playtester-returning**: Use to test Citybuilder as a returning player who loads an old save from before the latest economy changes and plays on: notices what changed, what confuses, and anything the migration broke. Plays on a Pro Max iPhone in portrait. ([cedricziel/citybuilder](https://github.com/cedricziel/citybuilder/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester-returning.md))
- **playtester-trader**: Use to test Citybuilder's ships, routes, market and rivals (and orders, once they exist) as a player who wants to make money by trading. Plays on an iPhone in landscape. ([cedricziel/citybuilder](https://github.com/cedricziel/citybuilder/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester-trader.md))
- **playtester-ui-auditor**: Use for a structured, device-by-device review of Citybuilder's interface: every screen on iPhone SE, iPhone and Pro Max (portrait and landscape), iPad 11-inch, Apple TV and Mac, against a checklist. Not a player persona. Writes one report per device and a cross-device matrix. ([cedricziel/citybuilder](https://github.com/cedricziel/citybuilder/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester-ui-auditor.md))
- **playtester-veteran**: Use to judge Citybuilder's depth, pacing, rivals and mid-game as someone who has played many island economy builders. Reviews the Mac app from window captures and plays hands-on on an iPad. Never names other games. ([cedricziel/citybuilder](https://github.com/cedricziel/citybuilder/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playtester-veteran.md))
- **playtester.agent**: QA agent that playtests the game inside Roblox Studio through the Studio MCP server, reads console output and reports bugs. Use after a feature passes `lune run check`. ([RustikArt/roblox-starter](https://github.com/RustikArt/roblox-starter/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/playtester.agent.md))
- **playthrough-reviewer**: Review Phase 5b planner changes and replay evidence for game-rule bypasses, recovery failures, and false acceptance. ([SteveLeve/daggorath](https://github.com/SteveLeve/daggorath/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playthrough-reviewer.md))
- **playwright**: Playwright testing agent. Only runs for FRONTEND tasks. Verifies acceptance criteria in a real browser using the page object pattern. Delegated by parent agents for UI inspection, interaction, debugging, and verification tasks. ([faroukBakari/trader-pro](https://github.com/faroukBakari/trader-pro/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/playwright.sub.agent.md) · ★6)

([abl030/nixosconfig](https://github.com/abl030/nixosconfig/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/playwright.md) · ★1)
